Reuse that work and its published semantics; do not repeat prompt-tail/cache-boundary implementation here. The new scope is measured elapsed-time joins, fewer serial transitions, attention cache invalidation, indexed plan/condition reads, retrieval deadlines and native evidence integrity. Our sampled inputs were already90–95% cached, so cold-start token-cache work is not a substitute for this plan.","date":null,"itemRefs":[],"affects":[],"lineNumber":1831,"provenance":{"kind":"unrecorded"}},{"id":"D-006","title":"One final acceptance join","body":"P-009 intentionally joins independent implementation lanes for the final matched quality/latency verdict. Recipe discovery drops stored input schemas, useful one-tool workflows are hidden, and capture-time matching must not be mistaken for deliberate saved replay.\",\n  \"outcome\": \"Representative agent tasks finish faster without losing correctness, and one existing session timing view explains model, native, hook, dispatch and handler time with explicit coverage. Existing recipes become directly callable and portable, decision-point recommendations expose current input/health evidence, and matched task benefits are verified through shipped artifacts.\",\n  \"nonGoals\": [\n    \"Changing models or reasoning effort as an unmeasured shortcut\",\n    \"Disabling coordination, locks, authority checks, semantic retrieval or verification\",\n    \"Treating every cancelled stream as a failure or attributing delays to resources without causal evidence\",\n    \"Reimplementing the completed October performance plan or creating a second tracing/cache service\",\n    \"Starting additional implementation workers or fleets: Avi selected this session/self. Existing independent acceptance reviewers remain required.\",\n    \"Duplicating the existing automatic-update, recipe-learning or native-cutover implementation lanes.\"\n  ],\n  \"sourceRefs\": [\n Reject incomparable or qu… <!-- P-009 -->\n## Measured basis and priority\n\nThe three selected Codex windows spent53–62% in model-only intervals,31–38% in tool-only intervals,2–6% with both active and0.8–3.4% in neither observed interval. The attention-serving process had11,000 invalidated misses out of11,005 misses,1,646 builds and52 deadline fallbacks. A legacy coverage query took27.970s and planned a full work_items payload Seq Scan; a condition-prefix history read took8.022s. Startup hook receipts were1.839/2.340s. These are bounded observations, not a universal regression or a CPU diagnosis.\n\nPriority is fewer unnecessary serial transitions plus the measured attention/SQL costs, supported by truthful attribution and serving-build checks. Existing October plan items remain historical and terminal; this plan addresses the evidenced November gaps.\n\n## Requirements\n\n**R-1 — Explain the actual elapsed interval.**\n```requirement\n{\"intent\":{\"request\":\"The existing session timeline exposes clipped native/gateway/phase intervals, exclusive categories, overlap, residual, source identities and data gaps. Bucket sums equal selected wall time within1ms. Known intervals explain at least95% at the model/tool boundary in the qualified current-build sample; inner instrumented server stages name at least90% of their elapsed time. Unavailable evidence remains unknown rather than being relabeled.\",\"sourceRefs\":[\"session_turn:codex:01a1b234-bb3d-7541-9d60-004d4b44b198:1\",\"session_turn:codex:01a1b234-bb3d-7541-9d60-004d4b44b198:9\"]},\"acceptance\":{\"condition\":\"The timeline reports a healthy LATENCY-M1 coverage verdict only when each frozen window meets95%model/tool union coverage with1ms partition tolerance and every declared current-build tool meets90%inner-stage coverage with unknown gaps preserved\",\"falsifier\":\"A yielded operation is truncated at its first response, concurrent spans are added twice, a corrupt or missing record becomes idle time, identities/builds are mixed, or a stated coverage threshold fails.\",\"evidencePlane\":\"live\",\"requiredScope\":[\"P-009\"],\"requiredTestLayers\":[\"integration\",\"unit\"]},\"verification\":{\"method\":\"Run maintained interval/identity tests, replay the three saved bounded windows as regression fixtures without prompt content, and bind current-build operational results and coverage to the existing results ledger. Add or extend the named conventional suite at every required layer; a planned path is not a passing result.\\n\\nMandatory operational protocol: The frozen LATENCY-M1 W1/W2/W3 population and inner four-tool population each meet the declared95%/90%union formulas with1ms arithmetic tolerance; P-001/P-007/P-008 supply only their local component proofs, while this complete coverage join is discharged only by P-009.\\nRead LATENCY-M1 and External adoption contracts from the exact subject-plan revision before executing. Resolve dev:pipeline_position for each source/testClass and verify loaded source closure, configuration and client/model/effort. Existing unit tests prove component logic only. Run the maintained live integration acceptance suite against the actual selected runtime, with strict LATENCY-M1 case selection, no mocked target runtime and no implicit skip. Assert the numeric/identity predicates from raw samples, preserve failed/aborted/missing attempts, and persist complete artifacts and source/runtime provenance through existing testing/results ledgers. For shipment use the dedicated shipped-artifact suite after promotion/deploy; staging proof cannot satisfy it.\\nEvery new or missing suite is PLANNED/UNKNOWN until it exists, is registered and executes the named population. The pre-implementation bound-file census has six absent suites: packages/operator-core/lib/agent-latency-acceptance.live.integration.test.ts, packages/operator-core/lib/agent-latency-shipment.live.integration.test.ts, packages/operator-core/lib/agent-tools/coordination/condition-state.integration.test.ts, packages/operator-core/lib/agent-tools/recipes/replay-contract.integration.test.ts, packages/operator-core/lib/agent-tools/sessions/timeline.integration.test.ts, packages/operator-core/lib/search/deadline.integration.test.ts. Each remains PLANNED/UNKNOWN until present, registered and executed on its required runtime. Recompute the bound-file existence census at each verification; file presence alone never proves a passing result. This is a required verification procedure, not a claim that tests or operational checks have passed. Grade from those exact ledger results.\\n\\nLATENCY-M1 revision2 oracle calibration: use the exact per-case qualityOracle from subject planrev18. T04/T05/T06/T09/T10 are predeclared evidence/diagnostic tasks: correct complete observed-versus-independent-reference evidence may pass a task while honestly identifying a baseline component failure. This never makes a component BAR healthy: the candidate must separately pass R-1/R-3/R-5/R-6 and every other required component predicate. Retrieval/lookup tasks still require correct complete results. Do not repair/substitute the frozen baseline, change an oracle after measurement, omit a failure or treat synthetic fixture timing as measured. Before baseline measurement, P-001 must pin deterministic UTF8 fixture bytes/SHA256, generator seed/source, independent reference and historical-card/plan/schema snapshots, the BASELINE source/build/runtime/configuration closure, tool schema/client/model/effort/cache setup and positive/negative oracle calibration. Persist the complete immutable baseline task receipts and obtain independent baseline acceptance BEFORE any product behavior edit. AFTER implementation and BEFORE candidate measurement, pin the CANDIDATE source/build/runtime/configuration closure in its own immutable receipt; reset the SAME frozen fixtures, references, schemas, cohort and oracles, keep client/model/effort and declared strata fixed, and repeat the unchanged calibration. Candidate code hashes are never prerequisites to baseline capture. Missing baseline evidence blocks product edits; missing candidate evidence blocks the candidate arm and final acceptance. Preserve every failed/aborted/missing attempt and all existing thresholds.\\n\\nLATENCY-M1 revision4 pre-measurement chronological amendment for WI-6554700010046321 supersedes ONLY the incompatible revision3 pairedTasks.order. Baseline phase: T01 through T10, cold then warm within each task, repetitions 1 through 3 within each stratum, baseline arm only (60 attempts). Candidate phase repeats the identical task/stratum/repetition order, candidate arm only (60 attempts). Match by task/stratum/repetition identity; preserve all 60 pairs/120 attempts. This phased design relinquishes AB/BA temporal counterbalancing and therefore retains temporal/environment confounding as an explicit limitation; it supports the declared matched pre/post verdict, not a randomized or counterbalanced causal claim. All prior oracle-calibration, component BAR, scope, quality, metric, 600000ms failure/abort cost, UNKNOWN missing-data, external adoption and exact-shipment obligations remain mandatory.\\n\\nLATENCY-M1 revision5 — cooperative qualification observer (pre-measurement; no acceptance outcome relaxation). {\\\"id\\\":\\\"cooperative-worker-census-v1\\\",\\\"scope\\\":\\\"Opt-in qualification apparatus only, extending existing nativeProcessTraceEnv loaderCensus enrollment and authenticated OUT. Passive diagnostics and unsupported paths never qualify complete closure. No new runner, queue, store, service or product flag; frozen product/input bytes stay unchanged.\\\",\\\"installation\\\":\\\"Before every application capture of each enumerated supported CJS/ESM worker_threads.Worker view, install a construct-preserving Proxy and syncBuiltinESMExports. Actual pre-application installation and complete supported-constructor-path census must be bound to each run. Previously captured, bypassed, internal/native/addon or otherwise unsupported construction paths remain UNKNOWN; absence of events never establishes support.\\\",\\\"closure\\\":\\\"Close OPEN to CLOSING, drain constructing to zero and atomically fix the admitted population as CLOSED. Keep measured intervals, operation lifetimes and overlap separate from causal hypotheses. Project native command/MCP events, declared hook receipts, model requests and dispatch/handler spans with owner/session/build identity, clock semantics and completeness. Never infer idle time from absent MCP records. No new public tool, service, table, cache or recurring job is proposed.\n\nFor execution efficiency, change how existing facts are gathered and reused. Batch independent reads through existing code:run/recipe rails; retain dependent decisions and work verification. Before any persona edit, read the canonical persona render/edit runbook and edit its canonical source, not generated copies.\n\nFor attention, reuse current L1/L2 and single-flight. Introduce semantically relevant invalidation on the existing event path; keep actual source updates and acknowledged states correct. For coverage and conditions, preserve the canonical result while replacing unbounded candidate discovery/history work with indexed bounded access. Any backfill or schema operation must declare its data/lock risk and use the platform snapshot mechanism before a destructive step.\n\n### Bar-to-work map for this plan\n\n| bar | implementing plan Typechecking alone is insufficient. Register changeKinds at implementation claim. Before live functional tests select the exact current-build route with dev:pipeline_position; use operator-api for these server readers, and the documented native route for client writer/hook changes. No source-only proof is called deployed.\n\nThe first controlled cohort holds model, effort, client/version, task acceptance and data snapshot constant, and records cache warm/cold strata. At least10 paired tasks are required; include all failures and aborted attempts with their actual dispositions. Separate request count, elapsed time, quality and p95. Preserve trace clocks and overlap; report residual instead of assigning it to model thinking or CPU.\n\nBind repeatable operational checks to the existing acceptance rubric/results ledger before grading; do not create a scheduled monitor as part of this plan. Final grading uses the canonical independent acceptance route. A final release verdict is coordinated with its existing live owner; this plan does not reserve or duplicate that lane.\n\n## Decisions\n\n### D-001 — Use the measured diagnosis as the baseline\n\nThe attribution report is the baseline for this draft. The plan targets its demonstrated costs and productizes its method; further instrumentation addresses the explicitly measured fine-grained coverage gap. Implementation and acceptance work remain open.\n\n### D-002 — No unmeasured model/effort or authority shortcut\n\nKeep the selected model and reasoning effort fixed in the first comparison. Reduce redundant orchestration without deleting useful decisions, checks, locks, authority or verification. Model/effort changes would need separate evidence and are outside this draft.\n\n### D-003 — Extend prior work and preserve semantics\n\nUse the existing timeline, tracing, cache, coverage, condition and retrieval surfaces. Keep the October performance plan's terminal records intact. No new public surface or recurring mechanism is needed.\n\n### D-004 — Localize claims to their evidence\n\nA UI cache miss pattern proves rebuild churn, not a global resource bottleneck. Native/gateway intervals prove where sampled time overlaps, not that all model time is thinking. Malformed records and unrecorded hook phases stay explicit. Target thresholds remain proposals until measured acceptance passes.\n\n### D-005 — Distinct from the existing prompt/token plan\n\nRead token-efficiency-prompts-cache-and-behavior-2026-10-11 before creating this draft. Its R-1–R-10 concern startup cache boundaries/relocation, a smaller repo guide, saved-result search, unchanged dead ends and their shipment evidence. Its remaining items are already built. Reuse that work and its published semantics; do not repeat prompt-tail/cache-boundary implementation here. The new scope is measured elapsed-time joins, fewer serial transitions, attention cache invalidation, indexed plan/condition reads, retrieval deadlines and native evidence integrity. Our sampled inputs were already90–95% cached, so cold-start token-cache work is not a substitute for this plan.\n\n\n### D-006 — One final acceptance join\n\nP-009 intentionally joins independent implementation lanes for the final matched quality/latency verdict. This fan-in is an acceptance boundary, not a serial implementation dependency: each lane runs focused checks as it progresses, and P-003 through P-006 need not wait for one another. Keep one final denominator and artifact identity instead of incomparable partial verdicts.\n\n\n### D-007 — AUTO and self execution through shipment\n\nAvi explicitly authorized the full consolidated scope and this session as implementer. Extend this canonical plan instead of forking a session plan; finish source review, activation and acceptance before claiming delivery.\n\n### D-008 — Reuse existing recipe, native and automatic-update owners\n\nP-010/P-011 implement the two concrete filed defects here. Broader overlapping work remains in its existing canonical learning/notification/native plans and claims; this session owns integration and independent preparatory steps. Do not call source-only browse eligibility a reproduced live first-page failure.\n\n### D-009 — Repair the complete acceptance contract before implementation admission\nDate: 2026-11-07\nBoth original independent whole-set critiques are now persisted: thread-post:1217360 (su6080d) and thread-post:1217366 (sub0c6d), each reviewing rev15/hash3e4dcd9470d790e068ee53a8fc000a77cd1332ec50cb0b6393906800be5d3088,17active clauses+18projections and15BARs. Both reject unchanged admission. Adopt their shared rulings: (1) distinguish tree semantics from live measurement and exact deployed shipment; (2) freeze complete measurement/recipe/journey/client populations, baseline, formulas, strata and failure dispositions before implementation; (3) keep global coverage/cohort/shipment joins on P-009 so lane-local completion never requires a downstream result; (4) make P-012/P-014/P-016 consumer/adoption-only where existing upstream owners retain implementation, pinning exact specs/artifacts/events or explicit unavailable dependencies; (5) name both recipe workflows and second-caller authorization/negative controls; (6) strengthen local clauses to their item-owned BAR outcomes and keep future/nonexistent suites UNKNOWN; (7) reconcile rubric subject rev13 with planrev15 and every meaning-changing revision. Use the canonical BAR amendment/requirement/spec tools, retain all existing terminal history and owner scope, then refresh discovery, source activation audit and the existing review threads before plans:start. This is an agent ruling from verified critiques, not invented owner approval or acceptance. No new parallel runner/store/service or implementation worker is authorized by this decision.\nRelated: P-001, P-002, P-007, P-008, P-009, P-010, P-011, P-012, P-014, P-016\n\n### D-010 — Acceptance BAR amendment\nDate: 2026-11-07\nAmendment-Id: latency-d009-complete-contract-v5-oracles-20261107\nRequest-Hash: 50fe51ef8fcca3343fd3a360eefdf46dfa6414b45a300120105c0226e62a8622\nActor: su-24790d3e-5b5a-41fa-a8b0-4faf7584ec9f\nReason: D-009 and independent critiques thread-post:1217360/thread-post:1217366: correct live/deployed planes; centralize global coverage/cohort/shipment on P-009; enforce frozen LATENCY-M1 populations/thresholds; use actual-runtime maintained integration assertions and exact artifact provenance; pin consumer-only external dependencies.
